## Repository guard predicates

- FLY-1560: retain `packages/teamlead/src/bridge/__tests__/fly1560-teardown-guard.test.ts`. Its literal guard walks all teamlead source/test TS/JS/MD outside fixtures, and its plugin assertions read a changed file directly (lines 143-190 and 46-60/93-121).
- Sparse StateStore migration: retain `packages/teamlead/src/__tests__/StateStore.fly663-migration.test.ts`. The three-column `codex_review_job` fixture opens StateStore twice (lines 70-105). StateStore is changed; this remains a bounded initialization/migration safety guard, although the correction itself changes publication transactions rather than migration SQL.
- FLY-2211: retain `packages/claude-runner/test/kill-path-inventory.test.ts`. The scanner walks `packages` and `scripts`, including tests, and inventories process mutation source lines; the production boundary registry names both changed TmuxAdapter and Blueprint. Candidate-preparation tests also introduce process-launch harness code, so the scan is retained.
- FLY-2567: exclude `packages/teamlead/src/__tests__/lead-token-savings-drift.test.ts` for this correction. `fixtures/fly2567/compatibility.json` contains 17 hashed members; intersection with the complete changed path set is empty. The guard only verifies those member hashes, not every StateStore/CommDB consumer. Recheck if any manifest member becomes changed.
- Always exclude `packages/core/test/tmux-viewer.macos.test.ts`: explicitly prohibited real GUI suite. The four retained `*.real-tmux.test.ts` files instead exercise the changed adapter through isolated tmux sockets/stubs; they are a separate bounded host-tmux lane and are not model/production acceptance.

## Bounded Vitest related command

Local CLI inspection: installed Vitest is `3.2.4`; `related --help` advertises `--exclude <glob>`. In local `dist/chunks/cac.Cb-PYCCB.js`, lines 1088-1092 define repeated exclusions as an array; lines 1379-1383 map related arguments and default empty selections to success; lines 1398-1400 transfer exclusions to `cliExclude`. `coverage.DL5VHqXY.js:3678` appends them to each resolved project exclusion list. The installed `cli-api.*.js:7862` retains a test when either its module path or a dependency is in `related`. A `parseCLI` read-only check confirmed two repeated exclusions and `--passWithNoTests=false` are parsed correctly.

Do not add test paths as positional filename filters to `related` and assume they bound the test universe: all those arguments are related sources. Use exact complementary `--exclude` entries first. Including the retained test files among related sources deliberately keeps source-scanning guards that have no static import edge. This yields exactly the intended candidate universe while preserving the existing package config, teamlead serial/parallel projects, setup files, model fixture, and CommDB isolation. No temporary config is needed.

Example below runs only the 65 retained teamlead files, with the changed teamlead source paths (including StateStore and workflow-template) supplied to `related`. Change `package` to another listed package to use that package’s exact retained set. The enumeration is from the filesystem, including ignored/untracked test-shaped files; this prevents an untracked test from accidentally escaping the complement. The current teamlead argv is approximately 96.6 KB and excludes 1,529 files. This example was constructed and parsed, not executed.

```python
from pathlib import Path
import os
import re
import subprocess

root = Path.cwd()
package = "packages/teamlead"
package_root = root / package
evidence = (root / "engineering/doc/FLY-2913-role-prefix/evidence/revision-test-consumers.md").read_text()
def block(marker):
    part = evidence.split(f"<!-- {marker}:start -->", 1)[1]
    return part.split("```text\n", 1)[1].split("\n```", 1)[0].splitlines()

test_name = re.compile(r"\.(?:test|spec)\.[cm]?[jt]sx?$")
keep = sorted(path[len(package) + 1:] for path in block("retained-candidates")
              if path.startswith(package + "/") and test_name.search(path))
assert keep and all((package_root / path).is_file() for path in keep)
sources = [path[len(package) + 1:] for path in block("changed-production")
           if path.startswith(package + "/")]
all_tests = []
for folder, directories, files in os.walk(package_root):
    directories[:] = [name for name in directories if name not in ("node_modules", "dist", ".git")]
    all_tests.extend((Path(folder) / name).relative_to(package_root).as_posix()
                     for name in files if test_name.search(name))
excluded = sorted(set(all_tests) - set(keep))
argv = ["node", "node_modules/vitest/vitest.mjs", "related", *sources, *keep,
        "--run", "--passWithNoTests=false", "--exclude", "**/tmux-viewer.macos.test.ts"]
for path in excluded:
    argv.extend(["--exclude", path])
subprocess.run(argv, cwd=package_root, check=True)
```

Separate exact commands after affected dependency builds:

```sh
node --test scripts/__tests__/prepare-2913-prefix-revisions.test.mjs
bash scripts/__tests__/qa-2913-prefix-controls.test.sh
node scripts/qa-fly2606-hot-config.mjs
```

The hot-config driver is an isolated temporary-database acceptance fixture with built package dependencies; do not substitute a production Bridge endpoint or production database. No aggregate package test command is required.
